##### C1 — strict durable state kernels and registered topology

Add `SpiritualWoundDecisionPendingState.cs` and
`SpiritualWoundOpportunityReceiptState.cs` under `BookOfEternityClient/Services`. Each type is a
write-free strict version-1 parser/serializer/reducer with detached values, duplicate-property and
unknown-field rejection, ordinal comparison, checked arithmetic, explicit bounds and recomputable
domain-separated fingerprints. Pending state owns at most one original-turn packet and may only
advance its immutable source order, staged decisions and cursor. Receipt state owns append-only
instance/closure/source/decision rows; terminal closure appends instead of mutating an instance.
A source append is valid only with one agreeing final decision, `none` has no transition identities
and `materialize` requires both.

Register both roots in `AfterlifeContractRegistry` as client-owned afterlife surfaces and in
`WoundAcceptedTurnSnapshotContract.RequiredPathInventory`. Add empty version-1 bootstrap/template
files only where the existing state bootstrap owns client-created roots. Update path inventories,
snapshot guards and strict contract tests in `AfterlifeContractRegistryTests.cs`, new state test
files and the existing accepted-turn snapshot tests. Because the registered pending/receipt paths
are afterlife runtime-contract surfaces, update `OtherGuides/Afterlife_Contract_Matrix.md`, the
`OtherGuides/Afterlife_Pending_Control_Surface_Inventory.json`, manifest and documentation/source
guards in this same unit to identify both as client-owned and
not GM-authored. RED first proves that the roots are unknown, not snapshot-covered and accept
malformed/colliding rows; GREEN closes those exact failures. This unit does not persist a packet,
parse GM input, create wound commands or publish state.

Verification: owning unit Focused selections, the required focused
`AfterlifeDocumentationCoverageTests` control and `FullValidation`, `git diff --check`, one Fast
control because production code changes, then independent XHigh review focused on closed-schema
parsing, identity collision, detachability, append-only behavior, limits, fingerprints and absence
handling.

##### C2 — pending capture, bounded decision waves and cold reconstruction

Add a persistence/reconstruction partial beside
`ValidationService.SpiritualOriginalTurnCapture.cs` and the existing source continuation files.
Only the retained capture owner may create the first packet. It copies the original pending-turn
identity, immutable validated source prefix, cursor, original dice claims, resource/effect/source
evidence, exact registered before/candidate images and preserved draft. A separate projection
produces visibility-safe GM context. Decision intake accepts only the current offered references
and closed `none|materialize` values, cannot alter frozen source or prior decisions, and advances at
most one bounded wave. Under the same active canonical lease, the capture is the sole unfinished-
packet writer: it may atomically replace only its own packet to advance generation/cursor, append
newly staged exact decisions and validated sources, and update owner-validated derived candidate
images, prefix fingerprints and explicitly permitted dependent draft fields. Original before-images,
dice claims, prior decisions and frozen sources remain immutable. This is resumable staging, not
accepted publication. Final pending
consumption remains exclusive to the common accepted plan.

Cold reconstruction starts from JSON bytes and a fresh service instance. It reopens the original
pending snapshot, recaptures canonical registered paths, rebuilds the retained prefix and ordinary
reduction through production validators, then compares every persisted witness and candidate
image. Persisted hashes are comparisons only. No parser/reconstructor returns the private live
owner without canonical reconstruction, and saving/resuming a packet produces no accepted receipt,
wound command, spend, notification, visible history or accepted-plan authority. Exact packet replay
returns the same stage; changed snapshot/source/image/dice/decision fails closed.

Use deterministic pure state tests in Fast and file/restart behavior in
`AfterlifeResourceCutoverTests.SpiritualOriginalPersistence.cs` under the appropriate integration
lane. RED proves in-memory-only continuation cannot cold resume and that altered persisted evidence
would otherwise be trusted. GREEN proves restart before a decision and after one staged decision,
including originally absent roots and immutable caller aliases. Run the smallest owning Focused
selections, the relevant `RegressionIntegration` selection for canonical file/restart behavior,
one Fast checkpoint, and independent XHigh review focused on origin reconstruction, visibility,
snapshot/lease freshness, one-use authority and rollback baseline preservation. Update the
afterlife turn guidance, daemon/launcher dispatch, one worked continuation example, manifest and
their guards in this same unit; the example describes the GM's bounded decision response while
keeping private packet fields client-owned. Run the required focused documentation control and
`FullValidation` before accepting this independently reviewed runtime unit.

##### C3 — accepted spiritual receipt and wound-decision reduction

Add a write-free reducer that consumes only a completed reconstructed capture, its exact
`CompletedOrdinaryMechanicsReduction`, the current durable receipt before-state and every explicit
staged decision. It derives or reuses the exact conflict instance, appends admitted sources and
decisions in causal order, projects the receipt-side consumption state and prepares the existing
common wound-command inputs. The first production milestone is an explicit `none`:
it appends real source/decision evidence and remains common-plan work even with no carrier/history
transition. The materialize path uses the existing common wound kernel and must agree on exactly
one decision, command result, carrier/index change and history transition. It cannot turn a lawful
materialize proposal into `none` or an unsupported-mechanic repair.

Source rows preserve the production-validated witness, including danger/escalation, actor/side,
art/tier, dice, resource/effect and optional re-trauma target evidence. Later exchanges reduce only
after the current exchange's decisions have been applied to the detached candidate. Decline does
not use the side's new-wound allowance. Exact replay returns existing rows and no command; changed
evidence under a reused coordinate conflicts. Unit RED/GREEN covers every reducer invariant. Signed
integration RED/GREEN covers real decline and one production-created materialize result plus
terminal/reused display IDs. T081-D remains the owner of lower/create/worsen/explicit older
re-trauma, simultaneous-side and dependent-later-exchange consequence behavior; T081-E remains the
owner of complete source families, danger/defeat/dissipation and truthful final preview. Their
outputs must cross this exact receipt boundary before overall US3 completion.

Update the afterlife matrix, wound/output contract guidance, worked example, manifest and guards in
this same unit with the accepted `none|materialize` receipt semantics. Do not expose receipt IDs or
private source witnesses to the player, and do not change the closed Mortal receipt contract.

Verification: owning state/reducer Focused selections, signed spiritual integration Focused,
the required focused documentation control and `FullValidation`, Fast, `git diff --check`, and

##### C4 — final assembly, sole common publication, restart and rollback

Add an owner-only completion boundary to the original capture that consumes C3's exact reduction
once and forwards its spiritual pending/receipt, wound command, carrier, effect, conflict and
history after-images into `AcceptedMechanicsPlanner.CompleteAcceptedReduction`. Extend final
assembly only where needed so a source/decision-only decline bypasses no-work early returns and
both new roots participate in touched paths, consumed pending paths, before-images and final plan
fingerprints. The planner registers one publishable `AcceptedMechanicsPlan`; there is no temporary
source-only cache entry or post-publication receipt writer.

Use the existing `GameEngine.ValidationAndRepair` and `GameEngine.TurnLifecycle` transaction for
write, read-back, visible history, notifications and rollback. Read-back verifies source, decision,
side, wound, effect and history agreement. Failure restores exact original bytes/existence and only
removes staging owned by this operation. A cold start after successful publication proves exact
receipt/plan agreement before consuming a leftover pending packet. Exact decline/materialize replay
produces no second spend, command, transition, notification, prose or ordinal.

RED/GREEN integration tests exercise live explicit decline and materialize through the production
common publisher, unchanged original dice/resource/progression identities, initially absent roots,
write failure, read-back failure, restart after publication and unchanged nonempty
`combatConditions[]`. Run the smallest owning Focused selections, targeted RegressionIntegration
and LifecycleIntegration controls for the changed boundary, the required focused documentation
control and `FullValidation`, then Fast. Independent XHigh review must inspect the common writer
receipts, live identity/history, current carrier image and canonical/full fingerprint semantics
before this unit can be accepted.

Update the afterlife lifecycle guidance, daemon/launcher prompt entrypoints, worked example,
manifest and guards in this same unit with sole-publication, read-back, rollback, final narrative
and notification behavior. Record why no new console/browser command or Mortal contract update is
required.

###### C4 concrete integration map — 2026-09-26

Read-only investigation after C3 implementation found three distinct boundaries:
the registry/cache normally rebuilds a plan, the normalizer explicitly rejects
live wound publication, and GameEngine output/snapshot checks recognize only an
initial WoundStageBundle. C2 currently has no production GameEngine caller.
Existing GameResponse.woundDecisions and StateDistributor's typed command route
are available vocabulary, not proof of a working spiritual continuation.

First bounded milestone T081-C4-A integrates the completed plan with the common
transaction; it does not claim live GM dispatch or completion of C4. Preserve
three separate image sets: signed pre-turn rollback, immutable original draft
provenance, and exact latest committed physical publication inputs. C2 cold
reconstruction intentionally ignores later uncommitted physical draft edits;
C4 must reject those edits before registration and again before taking a plan.
Do not replace original BeforeImages to satisfy the generic physical check.

Execution order for that milestone:
1. Add genuine signed C2/C3 publication RED tests through the existing normalizer
   transaction, for none and materialize. Pin current-draft drift, foreign/missing
   completion ownership, unchanged journal and zero writes on failed preflight.
2. Issue a private capture-owned publication handoff only from complete C3 and
   exact committed inputs. Bind the actual completed plan, receipt, original
   authority, publication inputs and authenticated rollback map. Register that
   result narrowly in AcceptedTurnAuthorityRegistry/AcceptedMechanicsPlanCache
   without invoking the ordinary planner again; preserve one-shot take,
   invalidation and complete fingerprint validation. A caller-supplied plan,
   hash or DTO must not grant this authority.
3. Add both spiritual private roots and client wound commands to the final
   consumed paths, including explicit none. Keep their exact current images for
   publication checks and signed original absence/bytes for turn rollback.
4. Extend CanonicalStateNormalizer.AcceptedMechanics/Wounds/Effects with the
   exact live handoff, retaining ordinary authority checks. Use the existing
   AcceptedTurnCanonicalStateRefresh in CanonicalStateNormalizer.MortalItems
   for writes/read-back/compensation; add no second writer. Verify receipt,
   source/side, live wound/effect/history agreement and consumed-root absence.
5. Test successful common publication and write/read-back failures, exact
   original absence restoration, and nonempty combat-condition siblings.
   Synchronize private-boundary guidance/examples/guards without advertising
   unfinished GM dispatch; run owning Focused, relevant publication regressions,
   XML, required docs/FullValidation, Fast and independent Astra XHigh review.

Then connect the actual same-turn GM continuation to the existing repair loop
and typed decision composer, extend GameEngine.ValidationAndRepair's existing
output/notification/snapshot route to live-only wounds, and prove cold restart
after successful publication without a second spend, write or notification.
Explicit none emits no acquisition notification. Whole C4 acceptance requires
these production callers and lifecycle tests; a private normalizer fixture alone
does not satisfy it. The exact repair packet/response routing must be specified
against the existing supported transport before edits to that boundary.

### C2 original-capture investigation, 2026-09-22 (#1536)

The original snapshot predates the distributed GM draft; mutable candidate after-images
cannot preserve its original unexecuted actions and independent siblings. The GameEngine
response builder is a lossy UI projection and consumes notifications, so it cannot supply
durable response authority. The ordinary narrative validator also normalizes by writing;
reconstruction needs a shared write-free validation seam preserving exact admitted bytes.

Effect/combatant/member and resource identity factories generate random IDs. Additional
resource definition/seal and pending-request callbacks allocate outside those factories;
resource pending creation records the current time. Cold replay must retain these actual
values without changing the existing random-opaque identity contract. Resource terminal
receipts already retain request authority and can be replayed through the real pending owner;
they must not be loaded as prevalidated final execution authority. Wound IDs remain under
their existing deterministic allocator.

Proposal C2-R1 in spec.md proposes a separate client-owned original-capture checkpoint,
ordinary allocation/time journal, retained continuation inputs and committed progress binding.
It leaves the original snapshot and current C1 pending format intact. It explicitly preserves
the existing portable client-owned-file trust boundary: current snapshot authority uses
SHA256-PAYLOAD-JSON, so no cryptographic authentication of arbitrary rewritten authority roots
is claimed. A random-seed alternative was rejected for this proposal because exposing the seed
would make future allocations predictable; a journal retains only actual allocated values.
The proposed commit point is atomic checkpoint replacement and exact read-back under the lease,
followed by derived pending publication/read-back. A failure after checkpoint commit requires
projection repair, never allocation replay as a new advancement. An uncertain write is resolved
against exact old/new checkpoint bytes; any third state fails closed. Active checkpoint/pending
bytes are excluded from recursive candidate images, while exact original before-images and
registered path membership remain required.

This is an unapproved proposal, not permission to implement the new root. After user review,
the accepted schema and commit/recovery protocol must be synchronized into data-model/contracts
and the C2-C4 plan/tasks, with consistency analysis before implementation. No runtime changes
or new test runs were made for this investigation; C1 verification above remains current.

the corrected text and confirmed `git diff --check`. C1 acceptance was separately verified against
stored test summaries. Next step: obtain user review of spec.md C2-R1 before implementing its new
durable contract; no tests/builds are running and no commit/stage/push/merge was performed.

bounded step; exporting comparison rows itself gives no right to persist or publish anything.

Live-binding prerequisite discovered during source call-chain audit: PreparedContinuation
PrepareCoreAsync projects candidate closure before validation can reject, while several callers
preserve the accepted capture for retry. A shared clock would retain rejected allocation A before
accepted B, so cold replay over accepted inputs alone would request B at A's row. Rolling back
only journal rows would leave an unjournaled memo hit on retry. Independent XHigh review confirmed
this at SpiritualWoundSourceTransaction.cs preparation and the capture's prepare/batch failures;
begin/resume/completion/opportunity/wound paths require the same audit before live binding.

T081-C2-R1-SPECULATIVE-ALLOCATIONS design: a private serialized disposable journal scope records
cursor, row count and key/identity indexes; one active scope, owner-bound single-use commit, normal
dispose rolls back an uncommitted healthy scope. Faults remain permanent and cleanup never revives

### C2 original input boundary, 2026-09-23 (#1536)

Sol High storage audit and Astra High cold-replay architecture audit independently established:
the current _observed dictionary preserves only mechanics reads, not the full distributed GM draft.
FileSystemManager has nonvirtual reads; replay therefore needs an explicit immutable candidate
input view within the same validator/fs/lease identity. Physical snapshot/current-request authority
must stay outside that view. A scratch validator or prevalidated snapshot override would transfer
or bypass authority and is not an implementation path. The original item/location/faction intake
must run again before resource/effect composition in a fresh service. Narrative/interface validators
currently write normalization; their pure cores must be shared with a write-free replay path.
These are implementation requirements of approved C2-R1, not new gameplay or accepted authority.

At the original capture entry, before raw resource/effect composition, reopen the real original
snapshot through PendingTurnSnapshotReader and retain its session/request/turn/snapshot identity.
Expose a detached declared original path inventory on its read result, distinct from the existing
selected CoveredLogicalPaths/AbsentLogicalPaths byte authority; do not enlarge the reader's
selection cap or claim newly authenticated absence for arbitrary dynamic paths. The draft inventory
unites declared original paths, current dynamic game_state/lore/world_profiles/output inputs and
registered fixed capture paths (including currently absent ones). Apply safe exact relative paths,
ordinal uniqueness and case-alias rejection. Exclude snapshot storage, manifest/authority,
rollback artifacts and active spiritual checkpoint/pending payloads from recursive input data;
their original before-images remain separate accepted-plan requirements. Read current input bytes
under the lease, preserve empty versus absent and never parse/reprint them. The original snapshot
is not modified. Freeze path order and defensive copies; a path outside this inventory is an error,
never an implicit read of the current filesystem. Retention alone is comparison data, not authority.

Only a successful CaptureAsync may attach the retained view, after its actual input/source tuple
matches the view's original identity and the observed inputs agree with the retained exact images.
Partition that comparison explicitly: ordinary draft paths compare against the retained input view;
excluded manifest/authority/checkpoint/pending and other excluded physical-control observations
compare against separately retained exact physical witnesses under the same lease and original
tuple. The latter are not candidate-read entries or recursively serialized input data. Do not skip
their freshness checks and do not call the view's unknown-path reader for them. Preserve the
existing original accepted before-images independently of both current-input observation sets.

Cold-intake fixture and typed-allocation design, pending concrete entrypoint wiring:

- Extend the existing signed conflict fixture through a pre-snapshot seeding callback. Seed item
  and location baselines before its original snapshot is signed; never patch signed baselines to
  make a replay fixture pass. Exercise actual raw NPC/faction, location and item owner intake, then
  resource/effect capture, using one allocation owner. These tests prove owner-intake reachability,
  not permission for arbitrary Mortal edits in a fully validated afterlife turn.
- Item creation coordinates include admitted creation reference, source turn, file/JSON path,
  carrier, route and source-authority coordinates. Journal the successfully issued unique ID;
  retain ordinary collision retries inside the allocation callback, and recheck uniqueness on
  strict replay. Do not request the same journal coordinate repeatedly for rejected random draws.
- Location/receipt coordinates use initial/materialization IDs, source turn, route and input
  operation path. Link/receipt additionally bind source and target references. Transition keys
  include kind, turn, entity, operation reference, source/target and governed child identity.
  Threat keys bind target location and its admitted threatsToAdd operation, not a global call count.
  Use the existing domain-separated canonical key hashing; keys grant no owner authority.
- Preserve actual ID prefixes itm_, loc_, mlocrec_, lnk_, mlinkrec_, mltrn_ and threat_. The current
  journal validates kind + underscore; new semantic families therefore require an explicit prefix
  mapping rather than changing gameplay IDs. Do not add bootstrap-reserved or normalizer-only
  producers to this raw-intake stream without evidence of an actual new allocation.
- Same-attempt repeated location planning must reuse the identical factory and complete input
  binding without duplicate rows. A new factory on the same fs/root must consume the retained
  stream again; a fresh ValidationService alone does not isolate the root-shared item cache.
  Strict replay uses throwing underlying allocation callbacks and rejects changed causal targets,
  reordered requests or collisions at the actual request. Missing/extra retained rows are checked
  at completed replay/export, after the saved prefix and exchange continuations run. Initial
  CaptureCoreAsync must not require full-stream exhaustion: the supplied journal may legitimately
  contain later continuation allocations. Do not invent a trusted intake cursor from saved data.
  Verify exact owner IDs, journal and unchanged canonical/snapshot bytes through the real owners.

The common core checks the lease and revokes the old capture/source. The new path additionally

Run shared ordinary raw location and item validators with explicit attempt factories; only with no

Attach only after the existing signed identity/freshness checks; commit the scope last. Never

### C2-R1 output projection implementation boundary (2026-09-23, #1536)

Source: approved spec.md C2-R1 items1,3,4,6; task T081-C2-R1-OUTPUT-PROJECTION.
This is an internal prerequisite within the approved scope. It changes no GM-authored schema,
player command, validation severity or accepted publication. Use Superpowers TDD/execution/review
against these canonical Spec Kit artifacts. Plan/tasks setup reused this feature without replacing
existing artifacts; optional commit hooks remain disabled by the user's no-commit instruction.

Files and interfaces:

- Create `BookOfEternityClient/Services/AcceptedTurnOutputProjector.cs` with immutable
  `AcceptedOutputProjection(string Json, bool Changed)` and static methods
  `ProjectNarrative(string json)`, `RequiresInterfaceTimestampFallback(string json)` and
  `ProjectInterface(string json, DateTimeOffset? fallbackTimestamp)`. Both Project methods return
  `AcceptedOutputProjection`. The interface method requires a supplied value only when the shared
  fallback predicate is true. Parsing failures return the unchanged input; clock callbacks never
  run inside a JsonException catch. The projector has no filesystem, clock or authority access.
- Existing normalization wrappers in `ValidationService.AcceptedTurnAndInkFeathers.cs` call the
  shared projector, acquire ordinary UTC time only when its predicate requires it, and write only
  when Changed is true. Move only the private normalization helpers/serializer with their exact
  logic. Preserve validator bodies, issue codes/severities, physical reader order, achievement/prose
  audits and decoded pre-normalization stale-output warning comparison. Do not add a second warning
  reader to the capture: it is a projection, not complete output admission. Full candidate admission
  will subsequently route these existing validation checks through an explicit reader.
- Add `InterfaceOutputTimestamp` to `AcceptedTurnProjectionTimeKind`. Use the existing journaled
  memo clock and utc_time family. Causal evidence is the exact original session/request/snapshot/
  turn tuple, fixed interface path and original image fingerprint. Generated timestamp is not part
  of its own key. No fallback or clock read occurs for an existing nonblank/nonstring timestamp.
- Create `ValidationService.SpiritualOriginalTurnOutputProjection.cs`. Retain immutable
  `SpiritualOriginalOutputProjection(AcceptedOutputProjection? Narrative,
  AcceptedOutputProjection? Interface)`; null means the original file was absent, whereas an empty
  file projects to an unchanged empty string. Decode detached original bytes with the same
  StreamReader UTF8/BOM-detection policy as FileSystemManager.ReadFileCoreAsync. Raw bytes remain
  in SpiritualOriginalDraftInputs without re-encoding or rewriting the snapshot.
- In the named intake CaptureCore only, build the projection after successful resource/source
  composition and original tuple matching, before final observed-image checks, owner attachment
  and the existing allocation-scope commit. This fixes timestamp request order before continuation
  allocations. Add both fixed output paths, including absence, to retained draft inventory, and
  only on this named path add them to observed freshness witnesses. Build from retained bytes,
  never re-read current output as the projection input. Final freshness comparison catches any
  changed file before attaching the capture; failure uses existing attempt/source cleanup.
- `ReadOriginalOutputProjectionAsync(lease)` is a gated memo accessor, not a lazy clock producer.
  Return `SpiritualOriginalOutputProjectionResult(SpiritualOriginalOutputProjection? Projection,
  IReadOnlyList<ValidationIssue> Issues)`. Under the capture gate, EnsureCurrent and check retained
  inputs before returning immutable projected values. Changed retained inputs return issues and
  revoke as usual; wrong/expired lease and revoked capture reject. A legacy capture without this
  projection rejects the accessor explicitly. Do not change existing CaptureAsync admission or
  allocate output time there. Later dependent candidate output must not replace this initial memo.

Read-only candidate-reader audit (cold_replay_architecture, Astra High, 2026-09-23): the next
coherent boundary is an explicit immutable current-draft view, separate from authenticated pre-turn
reads and real physical witnesses. Location intake reads map/current/index, NPC/faction/scaffold
through its helper; item intake also reads current carriers and its route/transfer catalogs open
files independently. Both catalogs mix current gameplay data with physical turn request and signed
manifest/snapshot reads, so a universal redirect is unsafe. Every nested location call must receive
the same view. Unknown draft paths must fail closed, never fall back to live state.

Later resource/effect routing must include commands, catalogs/history, full-party bytes, current
owner roots, world time, source roots and source-session candidate dictionaries. Same-turn effect
owner admission invokes selected ValidateGameStateAsync, actor completeness and faction validation;
these dependencies cannot validate physical roots while consuming different retained roots.
Wound-bearing effects additionally read raw wound commands/carriers/index/history. Current business
owner authority is candidate gameplay data; pending snapshot signing authority and lease/request
checks remain physical. General readers use BOM-aware decoding, source preparation strict UTF8;
retain bytes and preserve each decoder. Planning candidate images and actual publication witnesses
must not be conflated, or freshness checks become comparisons against the same in-memory value.

Before reader implementation, settle the exact constructor/tuple checks and staged private entry
for location/item admission. A test with intentionally different live and retained data proves the
selected raw owners consume the view; it does not authorize an existing capture to attach despite
its physical freshness mismatch. Full cold recovery still needs a checkpoint-authenticated original
view and independently captured real current witnesses, followed by complete resource/effect/source
routing. Do not make the existing final original-image checks tautological to obtain a passing test.
The audit changes no source and is not proof that the full named intake is cold-reconstructable.

### C2-R1 location/item current-input routing design (2026-09-23, #1536)

Task: T081-C2-R1-CANDIDATE-READ-VIEW-INTAKE, dependent on OUTPUT-PROJECTION. The private raw
intake must consume one immutable original input view while all original signing/lease/publication
checks continue to inspect actual authority. Reuse SpiritualOriginalDraftInputs; no new generic
filesystem abstraction, ambient override, scratch filesystem or deserialized owner is introduced.
This is implementation design only until independent consistency review and output acceptance.

Add a private ValidationService helper in the existing SpiritualOriginalTurnCapture partial:

```csharp
private Task<IReadOnlyList<ValidationIssue>> ValidateSpiritualOriginalLocationItemIntakeAsync(
    FileSystemManager.CanonicalWriteLease lease,
    SpiritualOriginalDraftInputs currentInputs,
    SpiritualOriginalAllocationOwner allocations,
    SpiritualWoundProjectionClock.Speculation allocationScope);
```

The allocation owner stays private. Named CaptureCore calls this helper instead of its inline
location/item sequence, passing the original frozen inputs and its existing combined speculation.
The helper neither attaches a capture nor commits a scope. Reflection integration fixtures may
exercise this same production helper, without adding a test-only accepted/publication entry.

Before any invalidation or admission: require the active real lease, healthy owner with non-null
Items/Locations, the read-only authority exclusion guard below, and no
`_prevalidatedPendingTurnSnapshotOverride`.
The current validated lookup honors that override, so it cannot silently stand in for physical
snapshot authority. Read PendingTurnSnapshotReader.ReadCurrent with the original soul path and
the ordinary physical validated lookup. Both must match currentInputs session/request/positive
turn/snapshot token (the validated manifest uses ManifestPayloadHash). Validate the complete
required current-input inventory up front, including absence. Reject mismatch before cache work.
Nested location and raw-item manifest checks also compare the view's four coordinates. Preserve
ordinary override support on ordinary paths; only this original-view entry requires actual lookup.

In SpiritualWoundProjectionClock.Speculation.cs add
`internal void EnsureActiveFor(SpiritualWoundProjectionClock expectedClock)`: reject null, a foreign
owner reference or a closed scope, then check the owner's journal usability. Do not invalidate a
foreign token/clock merely because it was supplied to the wrong helper. Existing construction owns
the sole journal scope; commit/dispose closes the token, and overlapping scopes already fault the
journal. No new clock-global active flag is required. Check the token before any intake/cache work
and before returning success, including zero-allocation plans. It certifies only the allocation
transaction, never acceptance or publication. The caller retains its existing commit/rollback duty.

Explicit current-input routing:

| File / path | Change | Physical/pre-turn reads preserved |
| --- | --- | --- |
| ValidationService.MortalLocationMaterialization.cs | Append optional `SpiritualOriginalDraftInputs? currentInputs = null` to raw core, raw file validator, raw plan method, ReadOptionalLocationObjectAsync, ReadMortalLocationValidationFileAsync and FileExistsForMortalLocationValidation; forward on every raw nested call | Validated lookup; snapshot map/current/index/storage/scaffold and pre-turn codex/regular quests/world events |
| ValidationService.MortalItemMaterialization.cs | Append the same optional view to raw core, LoadMortalItemCatalogAsync, ValidateRawOffscreenLocationStorageAuthorityAsync and ReadCurrentItemFileAsync; forward to nested location build, companions, current storage existence and separate player-removal projection read | Entire pre-turn catalog and backup projection-root reads |
| MortalItemRouteAuthorityCatalog.cs | Current-view overload/core routes its seven current roots through the view | Turn request, physical pending manifest and snapshot NPC/current-location/craft-request/trade-request reads |
| MortalItemAcceptedTransferCatalog.cs | Current-view overload/core routes inventory, NPC commands and player removals through the view | Authenticated previous catalog and turn supplied by raw item validator |
| SpiritualOriginalDraftInputs.cs | Add `ReadText(string path)` over detached ReadImage using MemoryStream + BOM-aware UTF8 StreamReader; absence returns null, present-empty returns empty, unknown throws | No filesystem or signing operations in the view |

Only when currentInputs is null do existing current-read helpers use their ordinary filesystem
branch. A supplied view requires an active lease; an absent/unregistered/erroring view read never
falls back to disk. Catalogs distinguish their current and physical reads explicitly: do not add
one redirecting helper used for both. Preserve each existing decoder, including later strict-UTF8
source consumers that are outside this unit. Ordinary normalized/post-seal callers omit the view.

Extend initial fixed capture inventory with the full raw current-input union: map, current location,
location identity index, bootstrap scaffold, NPC core, faction core, inventory, NPC inventory
commands, offscreen location storage contents, vehicles, item identity index, player removals,
item_bonds, item_text_updates, recipes, NPC item_journals and quest_history. Reuse existing constants
and MortalItemCompanionPaths. Fixed absent membership does not grant pre-turn snapshot authority
or increase the snapshot reader's selected-path limit. Existing physical snapshot/request and
final observed-byte checks remain physical. A different retained draft can be inspected by the
private raw helper, but cannot be used to make full CaptureCore ignore its original-image mismatch.

Keep retained treatment separate. Ordinary raw item admission retains CaptureRetainedTreatmentItem-
AdmissionAsync and its current lifecycle. View mode requires the private allocation owner and
the read-only exclusion guard before mutation; it never borrows that retained treatment path.
Its invalidation/cleanup uses exact expectedFactory: allocations.Items. The named CaptureCore
retains its existing initial broad revocation only after a treatment exclusion preflight; do not
add broader resets or clear reservations to support staged tests. Registration and nested caches
still require the same healthy
attempt factories, exact request/turn and original snapshot. Recheck lease/health before successful
handoff; an error after this attempt's item registration revokes only that factory's registration.

Independent design review identified why HasCommonValidated alone is insufficient: publication
take consumes common validation while leaving an open treatment receipt and resource hold. Item
Cache.Register also rotates its fence before allocations, so conditional cleanup cannot preserve
a previously valid foreign registration after replacement. Admission must reject these states
before mutation, rather than trying to restore old authority afterward.

Define the treatment exclusion under the root authority state's lock: no publication reservation,
reserved or publication fingerprint, validated handoff, open receipt, restart blocker or retained
publication lineage; the procedure dice, critical reaction and treatment resource registries must
all be empty. This is a conservative precondition for this original-intake entry, including local
finalized claim records; it does not change the ordinary treatment lifecycle or authorize clearing
those records. Check it before the named capture's existing initial revocation, and as part of the
private helper's preflight and final handoff check. The helper additionally rejects a validated
common plan and any raw validated item registration belonging to a different factory. Inspect raw
item validation plus exact factory identity under the item cache lock: ordinary HasValidated hides
private or faulted registrations. No validated registration, or this same healthy private factory,
is allowed; a foreign ordinary/private/faulted validated registration is not. These probes never
rotate fences, clear caches, consume receipts or release holds. Initial named capture otherwise
keeps its already established ordinary/common/item invalidation behavior; foreign-registration
preservation is the staged helper's pre-admission guarantee.

The guard must not mint a session generation: ordinary registry GetState calls
GetOrCreateSessionGeneration and can write a file. Add a narrow lease-checked FileSystemManager
reader over its existing ReadSessionGeneration policy, rejecting absence/malformed generation
without writes. Use RootSlots.TryGetValue and a locked noncreating slot accessor. A truly absent
slot with a valid physical generation has no in-memory claims to exclude; an existing slot must
match that exact physical generation, the root identity's revision and its bound authority state.
An existing stale/mismatched slot rejects, without GetForGeneration/GetState, replacement or
cleanup. IsCurrentSessionGeneration can check an expected generation without writes. The initial
treatment-only preflight is includeUpstreamIntake-only and precedes capture/source disposal as
well as broad invalidation. A guard is never an authority producer or permission to clear prior
state. Preserve ordinary paths and their existing generation creation behavior.

Verification sequence, before implementation:

1. Pure view text tests cover UTF8/UTF16 BOM, absent versus present-empty, exact retained bytes,
   detached aliases and unknown path rejection. Scope tests cover foreign/null/committed/disposed
   token and unchanged foreign owner health. Use owning XML Focused and meaningful RED assertions.
2. Signed intake fixture freezes valid draft A, then physical current data becomes B. Within the
   real combined scope, invoke the actual private helper and inspect private-factory owners/source
   exports/missing-governed/replaced-owner data, actual location planner/cache output, route/transfer
   results and catalog-derived projection roots. They must derive from A across nested location,
   NPC/faction/scaffold, offscreen presence, quest source and companion/removal branches. Do not
   obtain ordinary normalization snapshots: those remain forbidden for private registrations.
3. Include retained-absent/live-present and retained-present/live-absent cases; missing fixed path;
   each original tuple mismatch; changed physical signed snapshot/request; wrong/expired lease;
   override-backed lookup; absent factories; faulted cached owner; existing common/treatment
   publication, an open receipt with common validation false, a resource-only hold, finalized claims,
   missing physical
   generation, stale slot generation/revision, and foreign ordinary/private/faulted item registration;
   and null/foreign/closed scope
   on a zero-allocation plan. For preflight rejection assert allocations were not called and existing
   slot/fence/owners/receipt/hold remain unchanged; also cover a same-factory retry and a valid physical
   generation with no root slot. Failures before admission
   preserve unrelated authority, and later failures revoke only this exact factory. Assert ordinary
   getters/normalization remain unavailable and all filesystem bytes/inventory remain unchanged.
4. Keep signed existing full-capture freshness rejection and ordinary raw item/location/treatment
   controls. Search reflection and source-guard consumers of every altered signature before edits,
   preserving their real assertions when supplying new optional arguments. Run the smallest XML
   Focused and signed Integration selections, inspect actual diffs,
   then independent Astra XHigh code review. New filesystem fixtures belong in Integration. Fast

### C2-R1 raw wound-command applicability investigation (2026-09-23, #1536)

Task: T081-C2-R1-WOUND-DRAFT-APPLICABILITY,
[#1536](https://github.com/StanislavSmetaninSSM/The-Book-of-Eternity-Reborn/issues/1536).
Question: does the initial named spiritual intake currently admit any present
`wound_commands.json` that would require the raw wound-draft loader to read its current roots
from the retained A view? The loader is reachable when the command file is present, but a
successful spiritual original capture through it is not. A genuine signed Chaos Sea fixture
with wound snapshot coverage and an empty correctly bound command reached the loader and then
failed on `mortal_wound_validation_binding_invalid` from the downstream opportunity authority.
The observed Focused failure was recorded in `TestResults/test-lanes/20260923-182621-091-5100-e43d3e0c8caf431a975f266a1404430f-focused`;
it was a contract finding, not a production regression.

Independent Astra XHigh audit checked all command families. Empty and opportunity commands call
`MortalWoundOpportunityValidationAuthority.Reconstruct`, which requires `mortal_world` regardless
of command count. Treatment detached coordinates likewise require Mortal realm and must match
the accepted turn binding. Accepted-transition commands are rejected earlier as
`wound_command_transition_adapter_unavailable`. The spiritual decisions actually planned for
this feature use the separate source-bound selection and signed initial wound state. C2-R1 item 4
requires real owner replay, not acceptance of a command that existing owners reject.

Ruling: do not route this raw loader through named retained inputs until a separately tracked
valid spiritual wound-command adapter makes the path applicable. Preserve all realm checks.
The trial reader changes, fixture extension and failing test were removed; this investigation
leaves no production or test change. Future adapter work must revisit A/B current-view routing
before admitting such a command. No GM prompt, example, manifest or source-guard change follows
from this reachability finding; it changes no game capability or authored contract.

recheck GM docs/examples/manifests/source guards; private reader routing alone should not change

recorded here rather than silently weakening the validation set.

### C2-R1 durable checkpoint schema and write protocol (2026-09-23, #1536)

Task: T081-C2-R1-CHECKPOINT-SCHEMA under the approved T081-C2-PENDING-RECONSTRUCTION,
[#1536](https://github.com/StanislavSmetaninSSM/The-Book-of-Eternity-Reborn/issues/1536).
The completed candidate-read prerequisites now permit the C2-R1 data-model section 23.2
and the live-turn contract to define one closed private checkpoint. The registered service
path is `game_state/control/spiritual_wound_capture_checkpoint.json`; C1 pending remains
the same closed version-1 derived packet. Original signed snapshot authority, original
draft bytes, three immutable physical authority witnesses and original rollback before-images are
four distinct inputs. The checkpoint stores exact draft bytes/absence, separate excluded
witness fingerprints, an ordered replay journal, the initial derived pending fingerprint
and committed continuation input deltas with per-step decision/allocation/result markers.
No persisted row can authorize its own source, decision or canonical write.

Implementation sequence for the parent C2 task:

1. Register the checkpoint path alongside C1 pending in the private afterlife topology,
   the snapshot's closed original-path presence observation, common accepted-plan
   before-image/read-back/rollback coverage, manifest and guards. Select both service
   paths through `PendingTurnSnapshotPathSelection.CreateWithObservedOptionalPaths` so
   signed original absence remains valid; leave the reader's 64-path cap unchanged.
   Accept the earlier closed presence map for unrelated ordinary snapshot reads, while
   named C2 intake requires signed presence or absence for both service paths.
   A snapshot without both signed service before-images cannot enter this
   direct-cutover unfinished path. Preserve exact absence and reject a pre-existing active
   checkpoint for a different original request. Keep checkpoint/pending payloads out of the
   recursively retained GM draft and every repair-resubmission surface.
2. Add a strict detached checkpoint parser/writer and pure prefix-advance comparison.
   Tests first: malformed/duplicate/extra fields, path and case aliases, complete original
   inventory, present-empty versus absent, broken journal counts, reordered advances,
   changed decision suffix, candidate mutation through an alias, and digest mismatch.
   These tests establish only shape and monotonicity, not accepted authority. The parser
   bounds image arrays by the supplied draft inventory and journal counts by retained rows;
   it cannot derive the later accepted GM draft's exchange/source or owner allocation
   maxima from the pre-turn snapshot alone.
3. Build a write-free cold replayer on a fresh `ValidationService` with the same real
   filesystem and canonical lease. Reopen `PendingTurnSnapshotReader`; compare original
   identity and only the immutable manifest/authority/request witnesses. Mutable repair,
   ready, checkpoint and pending controls remain with their transport owner. Rerun named
   original location/item, resource/effect,
   source and wound intake against checkpoint A with `SpiritualWoundReplayJournal.CreateReplay`,
   then apply each saved continuation input delta using the production permitted-dependent
   edit check. At every step strictly validate the full rederived C1 packet, decision
   suffix and journal cursor, and compare its fingerprint with the stored step marker.
   Derive original accepted exchange/source and ordinary input bounds from these real
   owners; reject excess advance/decision rows before any recovery, repair or advancement.
   Exact replay and each saved cursor establish the allocation bound without an invented
   fixed cap.
   Compare only the final rederived packet structurally with an intact physical pending;
   never use that pending as replay input. Reconstruct committed A plus deltas before
   classifying later physical GM edits as a new uncommitted candidate. Reject before
   allocation callback, spend,
   write or accepted-plan registration on any disagreement. Signed integration tests prove
   cold restart before a decision and after one staged decision, changed original inputs,
   an originally absent dependent path and a forged self-consistent pending packet.
4. Give the retained capture the only unfinished-packet writer. Under one lease, finish
   the next bounded candidate in memory and export a healthy complete journal. Atomically
   replace checkpoint bytes, resolve uncertain outcome by exact old/new read-back, then
   replace/read back the derived pending. On old read-back discard all advanced speculative
   owners and strict-replay the prior committed state. If the old state was absent or
   `checkpoint:null`, preserve those exact original bytes/absence and start a fresh named
   original capture against the unchanged signed baseline; no checkpoint can be replayed.
   On new read-back with pending failure
   discard them and recover from the newly committed checkpoint. Any third image blocks
   continuation. If the second write fails, block new decisions until the cold replayer
   restores the last checkpoint projection. Inject failures before
   the first write, during an ambiguous first write, between writes and after the pending
   write; prove no duplicate IDs, resources, receipts or notifications.
5. Connect the visibility-safe GM wave and bounded decision intake to that retained
   capture, then have the common accepted plan alone consume both service roots together
   with receipt/wound/effect/resource after-images. Update afterlife GM matrix, guide,
   launcher/daemon dispatch, worked continuation example, manifest and source guards in
   this runtime unit. Verify ownership through owning Focused and targeted
   RegressionIntegration, required afterlife documentation Focused, conditional
   FullValidation for this affected documentation boundary, one Fast checkpoint and
   independent Astra XHigh review. PreMerge waits for actual merge readiness.

Each implementation step must leave T081-C2-PENDING-RECONSTRUCTION unchecked until full
cold recovery and common publication evidence is present. The schema design itself grants
no runtime persistence; its separate documentation review and scoped consistency check
are the current T081-C2-R1-CHECKPOINT-SCHEMA acceptance gate.

Schema checkpoint: a scoped Spec Kit consistency pass mapped approved C2-R1 items 1–7
and their acceptance cases to data-model section 23.2, this sequence and the parent C2 task;
the C1 pending shape, signed snapshot authority, original input view and common publisher
remain distinct. Constitution traceability and GM-sync rules are satisfied at this
design-only boundary: no runtime or GM-authored contract changed, and the runtime step
explicitly owns GM guidance, example, manifest and guard updates. Independent Astra XHigh
review found and then confirmed resolution of four concrete seams: lawful committed draft
B versus initial A, mutable transport controls versus immutable origin witnesses,
speculative owner disposal on failed checkpoint commit, and validation of each rederived
packet against its stored marker. It also confirmed the first-write absent/empty branch,
snapshot fingerprint producer and signed optional path selection. Final disposition PASS;
scoped `git diff --check` exit 0. This closes only the documentation design task.

Topology checkpoint 2026-09-23: T081-C2-R1-CHECKPOINT-TOPOLOGY registered the reserved private
checkpoint path in client-owned afterlife ownership, signed original-path presence, observed
optional service selection, accepted-plan path agreement and repair/status exclusions. New
snapshots emit 18 closed presence rows; the reader continues to accept the earlier exact
16-row set for unrelated ordinary reads, while named C2 intake requires signed observation
of both service paths. The afterlife matrix, pending/control inventory, worked turn example,
validation manifest, documentation/source guards and targeted tests now reflect that reserved
boundary without claiming a parser, writer or GM decision flow. The two stale Fast test
expectations found during verification were corrected to distinguish 16-row legacy reads
from 18-row production output and to bound the original-path source guard to its exact

### Approved action-cost execution plan — 2026-09-26 (#1536)

The owner approved the exact cost amendment in spec.md (original proposal
2026-09-20). Use Superpowers executing-plans and TDD against this canonical
plan, after the current T081-D receipt block passes its controls. No new effect
profile, resource pool, command, or wound-reaction graph is authorized.

1. Write owning tests in an Integration partial
   `AfterlifeResourceCutoverTests.SpiritualWoundSpecialCosts.cs`, alongside the
   existing ordinary action-point cutover tests in `AfterlifeResourceCutoverTests.cs`.
   Cover both player/opposition, no burden, missing/wrong audit, insufficient
   balance, failure, capped recovery and opposed recovery. A real current wound
   generation must supply the burden, not a fabricated scalar context.
2. Extend existing action-cost validation in
   `ValidationService.AfterlifeSpiritualConflict.cs` and retained-witness
   validation in `ValidationService.SpiritualRetainedPayloads.cs`. Recovery
   checks payment availability and computes ordinary recovery from the
   post-payment balance. Conditional force-incarnation audits use the approved
   0/0/0 convention; do not add a global mandatory cost entry that changes the
   unburdened legacy operation. Remove only these two unfinished-consumer
   rejections in `ValidationService.SpiritualWoundMechanics.cs` after their
   actual validators/resource consumers are installed.
3. Extend `AfterlifeSpiritualConflictResourceOutcome.ComposeSide` using the
   existing intents, source exports and expected transitions: for burdened
   recovery emit Spend then Gain with stable distinct operation keys and
   explicit predecessor ordering. Check positive, zero and negative net change;
   retain exact unburdened behavior. Trigger closure and use consumption remain
   with the existing resource/effect owners. Do not infer payment solely from
   the final balance or turn the two transitions into a net Gain.
4. Prove the common completed plan carries exact ordered history and final
   balance; include Spend-trigger behavior, capped Gain, unchanged closed
   prefix and retry without a second charge. Direct and retained receipt
   validation must agree. Preserve initial-stage/current-generation authority.
5. Synchronize the afterlife matrix, action-cost guide and worked example,
   example manifest and relevant source guards. Verify owning Focused,
   ordinary recovery/force compatibility, documentation Focused, conditional
   FullValidation, XML documentation build and one Fast checkpoint. Independent
   Astra XHigh reviews the complete block before acceptance; C4 publication
   remains separate.

Review focus: payment at zero balance; recovery near maximum; failed action with
negative net change; an opponent using a different actor; effects triggered by
Spend before Gain; old receipts with no burden; no duplicate charge on replay.
Spec Kit setup-plan/setup-tasks resolved the existing feature and preserved its
files. Optional commit hooks were not executed; no Git staging/commit is part of
this continuation. The two rules' approval is recorded, not implementation success.

Extend future shape comparison only for absent force audits; validate positive

operation/final-operation precedence and completed evidence. Update C2 dependent
failure classification, the opposition operation resolver and per-side resource

The same bounded consultation confirmed a second concrete integration seam:

### Terminal producer architecture and execution plan

Bounded Astra High consultation confirmed the ordering risk and this design:

- Keep AfterlifeResourceOwnerComposer's final authority unchanged: removed
  opposition is historical, with its existing deterministic terminal capacity.
  The retained source/capture derives a separate narrow execution view of that
  exact signed owner; no general permission to spend historical resources.
- Add a private prepared terminal-finalization capability, bound to the actual
  signed source/capture, complete validated resolution, original/final owner
  authorities and executor. It permits only the one pending terminal closure
  to pass completion preflight; it is not a receipt or completed proof.
- In the existing ResourceExecutionState.Run, prepare capacities once and defer
  only the exact authorized opposition Retire. Apply it using the same working
  state/history, allocation journal and sequence after the final exchange,
  causal effects and wound routing, before transcript/history freeze. Preserve
  other capacities and existing absence of resource-event triggers for capacity
  changes. Missing/foreign capability or incomplete work blocks Drain.
- Complete a private terminal proof only after actual retirement, final resource
  validation against the ordinary final owner authority, and effect completion.
  Bind exact original/final inputs, owners, terminal coordinate, retirement and
  transcript/plan identities. Discharge TerminalClosure only with this proof;
  cold reconstruction reexecutes/replays the same producer with fresh owners.
- Extend exact C1/C2 inventory and frozen resolution checks for the genuine
  single terminal suffix. Do not fabricate active B as authority. Source
  admission/continuation/completion probes must retain/revalidate the proof.
- C3 appends one closure using the existing strict parser/PlanAppend, immutable
  start row, actual instance, terminal event coordinate and exact resolution
  fingerprint. Common assembly must require the terminal receipt join even for
  all-none/no-insertion results, preventing a raw reduction bypass.

over general mutable flags, caller DTO authority or another executor.

in insertion order, including repeated worsening of the same wound; none and
guarantee_satisfied add no acquisition notification. Preserve existing one-time
notification consumption and session reset. This is an investigation finding,
not implemented output integration or a waiver of cold-after-success coverage.

Cold-after-publication investigation: C3 durable rows correlate the original
request/source/dice and wound transitions, but deliberately grant no common
publication authority and do not retain the entire publication/output footprint
or finalization status. C4-A consumes the private pair before TurnLifecycle
increments the turn/appends story; restart in that interval may see the old story
turn and surviving ready/snapshot. A matching C3 receipt can fence re-admission,
but cannot manufacture exact whole-turn completion authority. Full automatic
recovery still needs durable agreement with the complete publication and final
output, plus request-bound story/notification handling. Existing Mortal treatment
quarantine proves only its own typed-command replay, not this whole-turn state.
Keep this requirement open under C4; do not label the normalizer fixture or
in-memory notification exchange as cold-finalization proof. No recovery code or
new canonical schema has been implemented by this investigation.

### C4-B implementation plan — approved transport revision2, 2026-09-26 (#1536)

Use Superpowers executing-plans/TDD against this canonical plan and tasks.md.
The user approved spec.md C4-GM-TRANSPORT revision2 and previously selected
autonomous native implementation with optional bounded Sol High delegation and
mandatory separate Astra XHigh review. Preserve that method. Earlier pending
approval notes are historical; revision2 implementation is now authorized.

Goal: the existing signed spiritual turn can receive one exact current GM
decision, retain it across a dependent-result repair/restart, and hand the fully
completed C2/C3 result to C4-A. Architecture: the existing C2 owners remain the
only executors, the existing checkpoint owns uncommitted selection recovery,
and existing repair/worker transports carry closed comparison-only envelopes.
Tech: current .NET8/C#, PowerShell7 helper, existing JSON and canonical lease.
No new package, control root, HTTP endpoint, player command or second publisher.

Constraints: original snapshot/request/turn/dice/progression and closed siblings
stay immutable; no generic full-turn rollback/resubmission for a valid offer;
no lease across a GM wait; no user-visible private authority. Parent alone runs
builds/tests. Preserve the dirty worktree; no stage/commit/push/merge/issue closure.
A whole-turn crash-finalization capability is not supplied by a C3 receipt and
remains under parent C4; this plan must not claim that gap solved by transport.

Review focus and owning controls: B1 freezes an uncommitted decision even if
physical command bytes are replaced after restart; B1 covers automatic guarantee
satisfaction with no GM command; B2 rejects nested duplicate/unknown fields even
when the outer worker serializer is case-insensitive; B3 rechecks ownership after
worker apply before ready so a delayed response cannot overwrite a successor;
B3 rejects edits to an independent sibling within an otherwise permitted file.

#### B1 — durable selected decision before dependent repair

Files: SpiritualWoundCaptureCheckpointState.cs, SpiritualWoundReplayJournal*.cs,
ValidationService.SpiritualC2SavedDecisionDraft.cs/SavedTransport.cs/SavedReplay.cs/
PendingClassifier.cs/PrivateAdapter.cs, and a focused SpiritualC2PendingSubmission
partial if needed. Use the existing real dependent-exchange fixture in
BookOfEternityClient.IntegrationTests/AfterlifeResourceCutoverTests.SpiritualC2DependentExchange.cs;
new persistence/restart cases belong in SpiritualC2PendingSubmission.cs in that
test project. Pure closed-shape/reducer cases stay in the fast checkpoint tests.
Data-model.md defines the closed submission and transition comparison rules.

Split the gate-held selection and continuation into private
ApplyC2DecisionSelectionCoreAsync and ContinueC2SelectedDecisionCoreAsync seams.
StageAndResumeC2SubmissionCoreAsync persists the genuine selection before later
exchange execution; ReplayC2PendingSubmissionAsync reconstructs it under the
original owner. The session's ResumeDependentContinuationAsync(lease) accepts no new decision.
OriginalTurnCapture supplies the combined journal; InitialReplay, SavedReplay and
PendingClassifier support a pending selection at committed advance zero as well
as after committed layers. Never relax generic allocation replay limits.
Preserve AdvanceC2NextDecisionDraftAsync's write-free contract.

#### B2 — one strict transport envelope for file and worker routes

Create Services/SpiritualWoundContinuationProtocol.cs with comparison-only public
request/response/offer/field DTOs for the public worker models and an internal
strict parser/validator. Request fields and the response shape are exactly those
in approved spec.md; phase/cardinality and location/decision bounds come from
the rederived C2 request, never from the response. Parse JSON before loose outer
deserialization can discard duplicate or unknown nested fields. Preserve ordinary
outer contracts when the optional envelope is absent. Consumes B1 only at runtime;
pure protocol/worker structural tests can be implemented independently.

Modify GmWorkerModels.cs, GmWorkerContractValidator.cs and GmWorkerJson.cs only at
the new optional envelope boundary. GameEngine.PrivateImplementation.cs and

SpiritualWoundContinuationProtocolTests.cs and narrow worker contract cases.

## Test lane boundary plan — #1536 / #1505, approved 2026-09-30

Authority: `spec.md` TEST-LANE-BOUNDARY-DECISION revision1 and
`specs/1505-test-suite-performance/spec.md` FR-014/SC-013. This block changes
only test selection, result accounting and test documentation. No game or GM
contract changes. Existing fixture byte caches and four cutover shards remain;
the two compatibility corrections passed Focused2/2 in
`TestResults/test-lanes/20260930-040657-831-11356-034ad883d97349d09255f8775a2217c9-focused`, XML build zero warnings/errors, and independent Astra XHigh review found no issue.

The 2026-09-30 PreMerge `20260930-032548-791-29480-546ac749877745168a8f7bda05301a20`
ended after29:22 on two stale tests; 36/67 parallel TRX completed, including only
352/704 ordinary cutover cases. ProcessIntegration and E2E did not start.
Its9345 executed cases therefore are an incomplete-control count, not a timing
baseline for complete PreMerge. Historical separate controls: ProcessIntegration
555 cases in3:19 and E2E18 in1:28 on this machine (2026-09-04); rerun current
selections before relying on either duration.

### Selection design

In `scripts/test-csharp.ps1`, retain the old ordinary Integration category
boundary as a reusable filter. PreMerge's ordinary Integration selection excludes
the `AfterlifeResourceCutoverTests` class. A third parallel selection, using
that same category boundary and an exact method allowlist, restores these eight
one-case complete-path sentinels in one process so they can share process-local
prepared bytes:

1. `OriginalSpiritualCandidateReadView_UsesRetainedLocationAndItemInputs`
2. `OriginalSpiritualC1FirstOffer_UsesOwnedExchangeAndPreservesSignedOrigin`
3. `OriginalSpiritualC2SavedDecision_StagesOwnedWoundAndAllocationSuffix`
4. `OriginalSpiritualC2DependentContext_PreservesBaselineAfterCorrection`
5. `OriginalSpiritualC3Materialize_JoinsRealWoundToReceiptAndCommonPlan`
6. `SpiritualColdContinuationView_AcceptsDependentCorrectionOnlyThroughOwnerStep`
7. `OriginalSpiritualC4_PublishedDependentCostsRetainCausalityAndRejectDrift`
8. `ConflictFrame_DetachedApiExistsAndEvaluatesRealPublishedState`

`SpiritualCutoverIntegration` selects the 704 ordinary cutover cases with the
same original category exclusions. It retains the four balanced descriptors
and groups the five prepared-staged consumers in one process. The five cutover
ProcessIntegration cases remain only in their existing phase. Full-suite tests
must prove the exact 704-case inventory across four descriptors, eight distinct
PreMerge sentinels and five separate process cases; no method or case disappears.
Preserve ExplorerWeb's startup wave, Fast's seven-minute bound, four external
hosts, Fast's two-host cap, ProcessIntegration/E2E order and both existing
30-minute hard limits.

### Execution and evidence

First add a failing runner-boundary guard for the new lane, exact sentinels,
disjoint PreMerge selections, and planned-versus-completed accounting. Then
implement the selection and summary changes; a run that stops after only some
descriptors must say so explicitly and fail. Compare PlanOnly discovery with
the prior704 ordinary/5 process inventory and unchanged other selections.
Use Focused on the guard with XML docs enabled, the existing meaningful Fast
checkpoint, one complete `SpiritualCutoverIntegration` control and one complete
PreMerge including its ProcessIntegration/E2E phases. Inspect JSON/TRX/log,
exact planned and completed counts, no failures/skips/duplicates, and owned
cleanup. If either complete group exceeds30, measure the expensive descriptors
and optimize fixture reuse or divide the group again; do not silently raise the
limit. Independent Astra XHigh reviews the complete test-runner/doc block.
After both complete controls succeed, report measured wall time and stop for
owner inspection. The rest of #1536 remains suspended.
